#########################################################################
# The US intervention chain
#########################################################################

event = {
	id = 500
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_500_NAME
	desc = EVT_500_DESC
	style = 2

	trigger = {
		OR = {
			#atwar = JAP
			atwar = SOV
			atwar = GER
			atwar = VIC
			atwar = ENG
			#atwar = ITA
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = U08 value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = RUS value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = SOV value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = ENG value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = VIC value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = U09 value = 50 }
			belligerence = { country = OTT value = 50 }
			#belligerence = { country = ITA value = 50 }
			event = 2003010 #Anschluss
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = july year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_500_ACTA
		command = { type = relation which = GER value = -10 }
		command = { type = relation which = ITA value = -10 }
		command = { type = relation which = JAP value = -10 }
	}
}

#Early war - GER

event = {
	id = 501
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_501_NAME
	desc = EVT_501_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = GER }
			war = { country = VIC country = GER }
			war = { country = SOV country = GER }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = GER }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
		NOT = { event = 2003202 } #GER surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 50 } 
			belligerence = { country = ITA value = 50 } 
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		ai = no #currently only for player
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = GER }
			war = { country = VIC country = GER }
			war = { country = SOV country = GER }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = GER }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
		NOT = { event = 2003202 } #GER surrender
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 250 } 
			belligerence = { country = ITA value = 250 } 
			AND = {
				OR = {
					control = { province = 56 data = GER }
					alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
				}
				OR = {
					flag = ENG_invaded
					#USSR is losing the war
					NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } #Moscow
					NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } #Leningrad
					NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } #Stalingrad
				}
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_501_ACTA
		command = { type = dissent value = 8 } #Minimum 8 dissent
		#+ up to 92 dissent
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 100 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 100 } 
							control = { province = 485 data = GER }
							alliance = { country = POL country = GER }
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 150 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 150 } 
							control = { province = 56 data = GER }
							alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 200 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 200 } 
							AND = {
								OR = {
									control = { province = 56 data = GER }
									alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
								}
								OR = {
									#Balkans/Spain in Axis
									alliance = { country = SPA country = GER }
									alliance = { country = YUG country = GER }
									alliance = { country = GRE country = GER }
									alliance = { country = CRO country = GER }
									control = { province = 341 data = GER } #Madrid
									control = { province = 401 data = GER } #Athens
									control = { province = 401 data = ITA } #Athens
									control = { province = 382 data = GER } #Zagreb
									#USSR is invaded
									NOT = { control = { province = 212 data = SOV } } #Minsk
									NOT = { control = { province = 238 data = SOV } } #Kiev
								}
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = GER value = 250 } 
							belligerence = { country = ITA value = 250 } 
							AND = {
								OR = {
									control = { province = 56 data = GER }
									alliance = { country = VIC country = GER }
								}
								OR = {
									flag = ENG_invaded
									#USSR is losing the war
									NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } #Moscow
									NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } #Leningrad
									NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } #Stalingrad
								}
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		
		
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } } type = alliance which = ENG value = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 140 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 150 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 160 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 170 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 180 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 190 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = GER } } } type = war which = GER }
	}
}

#Early war - JAP

event = {
	id = 502
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_502_NAME
	desc = EVT_502_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
			war = { country = VIC country = JAP }
			war = { country = SOV country = JAP }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = JAP }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		belligerence = { country = JAP value = 50 } 
	}
	trigger = {
		ai = no #currently only for player
		OR = {
			war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
			war = { country = VIC country = JAP }
			war = { country = SOV country = JAP }
		}
		NOT = {
			war = { country = USA country = JAP }
			war = { country = USA country = ENG }
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 250 } 
			AND = { 
				exists = U87
				NOT = { exists = CHI }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
				#China is losing the war
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 65  }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
				war = { country = ENG country = GER }
			}
			AND = {
				#invading USSR
				OR = {
					control = { province = 1372 data = JAP } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1372 data = MAN } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1366 data = JAP } #Khabarovsk
					control = { province = 1366 data = MAN } #Khabarovsk
					control = { province = 1520 data = JAP } #Chita
					control = { province = 1520 data = MAN } #Chita
					control = { province = 1404 data = JAP } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MAN } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MEN } #Ulanbataar
				}
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_502_ACTA
		command = { type = dissent value = 8 } #Minimum 8 dissent
		#+ up to 92 dissent
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 35  }
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 150 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 45  }
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 200 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								#China is losing the war
								flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 55  }
							}
							control = { province = 1337 data = JAP }
							alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP }
							AND = {
								war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
								war = { country = ENG country = GER }
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		command = { trigger = {
						NOT = {
							belligerence = { country = JAP value = 250 } 
							AND = { 
								exists = U87
								NOT = { exists = CHI }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
								#China is losing the war
								flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 65  }
							}
							AND = {
								war = { country = ENG country = JAP }
								war = { country = ENG country = GER }
							}
							AND = {
								#invading USSR
								OR = {
									control = { province = 1372 data = JAP } #Vladivostok
									control = { province = 1372 data = MAN } #Vladivostok
									control = { province = 1366 data = JAP } #Khabarovsk
									control = { province = 1366 data = MAN } #Khabarovsk
									control = { province = 1520 data = JAP } #Chita
									control = { province = 1520 data = MAN } #Chita
									control = { province = 1404 data = JAP } #Ulanbataar
									control = { province = 1404 data = MAN } #Ulanbataar
									control = { province = 1404 data = MEN } #Ulanbataar
								}
								belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
							}
						}
					}
		type = dissent value = 23 }
		
		
		command = { trigger = { war = { country = JAP country = ENG } }	type = alliance which = ENG value = 1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 100 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 110 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 120 } } type = dissent value = 2  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = SOV value = 130 } } type = dissent value = 2  }

# Squeezing Japan
# 1 (Pitman Act)

event = {
	id = 510
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_84_NAME
	desc = EVT_510_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 90 } #3 months
			}
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 40 } 
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 20 }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 90 } #3 months
			}
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 40 } 
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 20 }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_510_ACTA
		command = { 																											type = dissent value = 0.2 }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value =  90 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value =  90 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 100 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 110 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 120 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 130 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 190 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		
		command = { type = supplies value = -1500 }
		command = { type = oilpool value = -250 }		
		command = { type = money value = 50 }

# 2 (Moral Embargo)

event = {
	id = 520
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_520_NAME
	desc = EVT_520_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 510
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 180 } #no less than 6 months
			}
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			event = 2049019 #Panay incident, Japan refused to apologize
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 360 } #1 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 27 }
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR cancelled aid?
			control = { province = 7027 data = JAP } #North Indochina
			control = { province = 1337 data = JAP } #South Indochina
			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		event = 510
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 180 } #no less than 6 months
			}
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
		OR = {
			event = 2049019 #Panay incident, Japan refused to apologize
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			event = { id = 1330110081 days = 360 } #1 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 27 }
			NOT = { flag = OperationZ } #USSR cancelled aid?
			control = { province = 7027 data = JAP } #North Indochina
			control = { province = 1337 data = JAP } #South Indochina
			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_511_ACTA
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } } 												type = dissent value = 0.2 } #arms industry and isolationists are upset
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 100 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 270 } #no less than 9 months
			}
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
	}

# 6 (Oil Embargo)

event = {
	id = 560
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_560_NAME
	desc = EVT_560_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 540 #can skip lend lease if China is beaten
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 540 } #no less than 18 months
				event = 550
			}
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 150 } 
			#event = { id = 1330110081 days = 1800 } #5 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 55 }
			control = { province = 1337 data = JAP } #South Indochina
			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
			}
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		event = 540 #can skip lend lease if China is beaten
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				event = { id = 1330110081 days = 540 } #no less than 18 months
				event = 550
			}
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 150 } 
			#event = { id = 1330110081 days = 1800 } #5 year anniversary
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 55 }

			alliance = { country = U03 country = JAP } #Puppet Indochina

			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_560_ACTA
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } } 												type = dissent value = 1  } #industrialists and isolationists are upset
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 180 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 190 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = SOV value = 160 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

# 6 (US DOW Japan)

event = {
	id = 570
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_570_NAME
	desc = EVT_570_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 560
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = HOL }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
			event = { id = 560 days = 90 } #no less than 3 months from oil embargo
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = JAP } }
			#flag = GER_USA_undeclared_war
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		NOT = { non_aggression = { country = JAP country = USA } }
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 280 } 
			war = { country = JAP }
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
				war = { country = GER country = ENG }
			}
			AND = {
				war = { country = JAP country = SOV }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
			}
			#Japan getting close to victory in China despite the aid
			flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 55  }
			#Japan overrunning the main supply route from India
			area = { area = "Yunnan" country = JAP data = 1 value = 70 } 
			area = { area = "Yunnan" country = U87 data = 1 value = 70 }
			AND = {
				#invading USSR
				OR = {
					control = { province = 1372 data = JAP } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1372 data = MAN } #Vladivostok
					control = { province = 1366 data = JAP } #Khabarovsk
					control = { province = 1366 data = MAN } #Khabarovsk
					control = { province = 1520 data = JAP } #Chita
					control = { province = 1520 data = MAN } #Chita
					control = { province = 1404 data = JAP } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MAN } #Ulanbataar
					control = { province = 1404 data = MEN } #Ulanbataar
				}
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			}
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		event = 560
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = HOL }
			war = { country = JAP country = SOV }

event = {
	id = 590
	random = no
	country = SOV
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_590_NAME
	desc = EVT_590_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP } }
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
			war = { country = JAP country = CHC }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = USA }
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			AND = {
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = JAP } }
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			}
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP } }
		OR = {
			war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
			war = { country = JAP country = CHC }
			war = { country = JAP country = ENG }
			war = { country = JAP country = USA }
		}
		NOT = { event = 2049007 } #JAP surrender
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = JAP value = 100 } 
			AND = {
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = SOV country = JAP } }
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 70 } 
			}
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } }
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }
		}
		OR = {
			#japan is close to surrender
			nuked = { country = JAP data = -3 }
			lost_national = { country = JAP value = 20 } 
		
			OR = {
				belligerence = { country = JAP value = 350 } 
				AND = {
					belligerence = { country = JAP value = 300 } 
					NOT = { event = 2003202 } #Potsdam
				}
			}

				flag = OperationZ
				OR = {
					#Japan getting close to victory in China despite the aid

					area = { area = "Shaanxi" country = U87 data = 1 value = 70 }

					flag = LanzhouRoadClosed
				}
			}
			
			
			AND = {
				#Japan has almost no defenses on the border. Unfortunately, cannot count allied troops, counting only Japanese
				NOT = { ai = JAP }
				NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 1379 type = land size = 12 area = 3 } } #Manchuria
				NOT = { garrison = { country = JAP province = 1199 type = land size = 15 area = 2 } } #Korea
				war = { country = JAP country = ENG } #Japan distracted
				war = { country = JAP country = USA }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				#USSR has plenty of troops on the border
				garrison = { country = SOV province = 1525 type = land size = 15 area = 2 }
			}
		}
		
		#control = { province = 123 data = SOV } #Terijoki (Finland defeated OR gave in)
		NOT = { 
			#war = { country = GER }
			war = { country = JAP }
			war = { country = ENG }
			#war = { country = FIN }
			#war = { country = SWE }
			#war = { country = ROM }
		}
	
		NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 10 } }
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
			# Finished reogranization of the Red Army (~Nov 1942) and ready to strike
			event = { id = 2007030 days = 30 } 
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 180 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { belligerence = { country = ENG value = 190 } NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } event = 2003202 } } type = dissent value = 1  }
		
		command = { type = belligerence value = 1 }

event = {
	id = 610
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_610_NAME
	desc = EVT_610_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }

			war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
			flag = GER_USA_undeclared_war
		}
		NOT = { event = 2003202 } #GER surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 150 } 
			NOT = { region = { region = "Poland" country = POL data = 1 value = 60 } }
			event = 2002008 #French State
			NOT = { war = { country = VIC country = GER } }
			#Balkans/Spain in Axis
			alliance = { country = SPA country = GER }
			alliance = { country = YUG country = GER }
			alliance = { country = GRE country = GER }
			alliance = { country = CRO country = GER }
			control = { province = 341 data = GER } #Madrid
			control = { province = 401 data = GER } #Athens
			control = { province = 401 data = ITA } #Athens
			control = { province = 382 data = GER } #Zagreb
			#USSR is invaded
			AND = {
				war = { country = SOV country = GER }
				under_attack = SOV
			}
			war = { country = GER }
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }
			war = { country = GER country = VIC }
			war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		}
		OR = {
			NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
			flag = GER_USA_undeclared_war
		}
		NOT = { event = 2003202 } #GER surrender
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 150 } 
			NOT = { region = { region = "Poland" country = POL data = 1 value = 60 } }
			event = 2002008 #French State
			NOT = { war = { country = VIC country = GER } }
			#Balkans/Spain in Axis
			alliance = { country = SPA country = GER }
			alliance = { country = YUG country = GER }
			alliance = { country = GRE country = GER }
			alliance = { country = CRO country = GER }
			control = { province = 341 data = GER } #Madrid
			control = { province = 401 data = GER } #Athens
			control = { province = 401 data = ITA } #Athens
			control = { province = 382 data = GER } #Zagreb
			#USSR is invaded
			AND = {
				war = { country = SOV country = GER }
				under_attack = SOV
			}
			war = { country = GER }
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_610_ACTA

	country = USA

	desc = EVT_620_DESC

	desc = EVT_630_DESC

		}

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = GER } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = GER } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = USA country = GER } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

		command = { type = end_trades which = USA where = ITA }

# 4 (Undeclared War)

event = {
	id = 640
	random = no
	country = USA
	save_date = yes

	name = EVT_640_NAME
	desc = EVT_640_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		OR = {
			war = { country = GER country = ENG }
			war = { country = GER country = VIC }
			war = { country = GER country = SOV }
		}
		NOT = { war = { country = GER } }
		event = 630
		NOT = { event = 2003202 } #GER surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 270 } 
			flag = ENG_invaded
			#USSR is losing a lot of land
			AND = {
				war = { country = SOV country = GER }
				lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 22 }
				#NOT = { control = { province = 212 data = SOV } } #Minsk
				#NOT = { control = { province = 238 data = SOV } } #Kiev
			}
			AND = {
				event = { id = 630 days = 210 } #7 months after Lend-Lease
				OR = {
					war = { country = JAP }
					NOT = { flag = NOUSW_GER }
					AND = {

event = {
	id = 650
	random = no
	country = USA

	name = EVT_650_NAME
	desc = EVT_650_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 640
		NOT = { event = 2003202 } #GER surrender
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		flag = GER_USA_undeclared_war
		event = { id = 640 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 320 } 
			flag = ENG_invaded
			AND = {
				event = { id = 640 days = 180 } #6 months after Undeclared War, Germany is still pursuing USW
				OR = {
					NOT = { flag = NOUSW_GER }
					AND = {
						war = { country = ENG country = ITA }
						NOT = { flag = NOUSW_ITA }
					}
					#roughly everything west of the 26th meridian
					garrison = { country = GER province = 6580 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 6576 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 6905 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 7470 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 2315 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 2355 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 7493 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 4386 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = GER province = 2542 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 6580 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 6576 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 6905 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 7470 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 2315 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 2355 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 7493 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 4386 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
					garrison = { country = ITA province = 2542 type = naval size = 3 area = 2 } 
				}
			}
			#USSR is losing the war
			NOT = { control = { province = 175 data = SOV } } #Moscow
			NOT = { control = { province = 187 data = SOV } } #Leningrad
			NOT = { control = { province = 163 data = SOV } } #Stalingrad

			event = 2001251 #Germany rejected British peace offer
		}
	}

		event = 640
		NOT = { event = 2003202 } #GER surrender
		flag = GER_USA_undeclared_war
		event = { id = 640 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			belligerence = { country = GER value = 320 } 
			flag = ENG_invaded
			AND = {
				event = { id = 640 days = 180 } #6 months after Undeclared War, Germany is still pursuing USW

	country = SOV
	save_date = yes

	desc = EVT_750_DESC

  	picture = "ccip_ccpsov"

		NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = SOV } }

			war = { country = GER }

				war = { country = JAP }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = MAN country = SOV } }
			}
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = 2012081
		non_aggression = { country = CHI country = SOV }
		NOT = { non_aggression = { country = JAP country = SOV } }

		}
		supplies = 6000
	}

		war = { country = CHI country = JAP }
		NOT = { war = { country = CHI country = SOV } }
		NOT = { 
			war = { country = GER }

				war = { country = JAP }
				NOT = { non_aggression = { country = MAN country = SOV } }
			}

		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 120 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 130 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 140 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 150 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 160 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }
		command = { trigger = { NOT = { war = { country = SOV country = JAP } } belligerence = { country = ENG value = 170 } }	type = dissent value = 1  }

#Soviet-Japanese non-aggression pact
event = {
	id = 1012
	random = no
	country = SOV

	name = EVT_1012_NAME
	desc = EVT_1012_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		NOT = { flag = JAP_SOV_NAP }
		event = 2011024 #Chasan lake
		event = 2011023 #Khalkin Ghol
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = SOV } }
		#Human SOV has to have at least 30 divisions in the far east region, otherwise Japan is not interested
		garrison = { country = SOV province = 1372 type = land size = 30 area = 2 }
		OR = {
			NOT = { ai = JAP }
			AND = {
				event = 2011014 #Khalkin Ghol - Soviet Victory
				event = 540 #US export control act - war against the US is looming
			}
		}
		OR = {
			event = 2011016 #Khalkin Ghol - Japanese Victory
			NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = CHI } }
			NOT = { alliance = { country = CHC country = CHI } }
			event = 1330110180 # NewFourthArmyIncident
			lost_national = { country = SOV value = 10 }
			war = { country = SOV country = GER }
			event = { id = 2002008 days = 180 } #~6 months since the fall of France. The USSR is getting worried about the looming German invasion
			AND = {
				event = 2002008
				flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 #USA is supporting China
			}
		}
	}
	trigger = {
		NOT = { flag = JAP_SOV_NAP }
		event = 2011024 #Chasan lake
		event = 2011023 #Khalkin Ghol
		NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = SOV } }
		OR = {
			NOT = { ai = JAP }
			AND = {
				event = 2011014 #Khalkin Ghol - Soviet Victory
				event = 540 #US export control act - war against the US is looming
				NOT = { flag = { which = japChi when = 1 value = 60  } }
			}
		}
		OR = {
			event = 2011016 #Khalkin Ghol - Japanese Victory
			NOT = { war = { country = JAP country = CHI } }
			NOT = { alliance = { country = CHC country = CHI } }
			event = 1330110180 # NewFourthArmyIncident
			lost_national = { country = SOV value = 10 }
			war = { country = SOV country = GER }
			event = { id = 2002008 days = 180 } #~6 months since the fall of France. The USSR is getting worried about the looming German invasion
			AND = {
				event = 2002008
				flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 #USA is supporting China
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 30
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_1012_ACTA
		command = { type = trigger which = 1013 }
	}
}

#UK aid to China adjustment/termination

event = {
	id = 1020
	random = no
	country = ENG
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_1020_NAME
	desc = EVT_1020_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 810
		OR = {
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI0
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI3
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI4
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 810 days = 30 }
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI0
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI3
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI4
		}
		event = { id = 810 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 35 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 35 }
				flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 45 }
				NOT = { flag = BurmaRoadClosed }

				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI3 }
				war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				flag = { which = japChi when = 1	value = 45 }
				flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 50 }

		name = EVT_1000_LOW

			NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 }
			OR = {
				ai = no
				AND = {
					war = { country = JAP country = CHI }
					NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
					flag = { which = japChi when = -2	value = 35 }
				}
			}
		}
		
		command = { type = end_trades which = ENG where = CHI }
		
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 } type = belligerence value = -1  }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 } type = relation 	which = CHI value = -10 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 } type = relation 	which = JAP value =  10 }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 } type = clrflag 		which = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI1 }
		
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 } type = belligerence value = -2  }
		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 } type = free_ic  	value = 3   }

		command = { trigger = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_CHI2 } type = relation 	which = JAP value =  20 }

#US aid to UK adjustment/termination

event = {
	id = 1030
	random = no
	country = USA
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_1030_NAME
	desc = EVT_1030_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 630
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG0
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG2
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG3
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG4
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 630 days = 30 }
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG0
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG2
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG3
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG4
		}
		event = { id = 630 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1 }
				war = { country = GER country = ENG }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				NOT = { flag = ENG_invaded }
				lost_national = { country = GER value = 15 }
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG2 }
				war = { country = GER country = ENG }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				NOT = { flag = ENG_invaded }
				NOT = { lost_national = { country = GER value = 15 } }
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG3 }
				war = { country = GER country = ENG }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				region = { region = "England" country = ENG data = 1 value = 40 }
				OR = {
					flag = ENG_invaded
					flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
				}
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG4 }
				war = { country = GER country = ENG }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				OR = {
					NOT = { region = { region = "England" country = ENG data = 1 value = 40 } }
					flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
					flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
				}
			}
			AND = {
				OR = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG0 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG2 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG3 flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG4 }
				OR = { 
					NOT = { war = { country = GER country = ENG } }
					lost_national = { value = 10 }
				}
			}
		}
	}

	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }
	offset = 60
	deathdate = { day = 29 month = december year = 1963 }

	action = {
		name = EVT_1000_LOW
		trigger = {
			NOT = { flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_ENG1 }
			OR = {
				ai = no
				AND = {
					war = { country = GER country = ENG }
					NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
					NOT = { flag = ENG_invaded }
					lost_national = { country = GER value = 15 }
					NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 }
				}
			}
		}

event = {
	id = 1040
	random = no
	country = USA
	persistent = yes

	name = EVT_1040_NAME
	desc = EVT_1040_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 710
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 710 days = 30 }
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
			flag = USA_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
		event = { id = 710 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			AND = {

	random = no
	country = ENG
	persistent = yes

	desc = EVT_1050_DESC
	style = 2

	decision = {
		event = 730
		OR = {
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
	}
	decision_trigger = {
		event = { id = 730 days = 30 }
	}
	trigger = {
		OR = {
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV0
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV3
			flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV4
		}
		event = { id = 730 days = 30 }
		OR = {
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV1 }
				NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }

				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 5 } }
			}
			AND = {
				NOT = { flag = ENG_Lend_Lease_to_SOV2 }
				NOT = { flag = SOV_noLendLease }
				war = { country = GER country = SOV }
				NOT = { lost_national = { value = 10 } }
				lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 5 }
				NOT = { lost_VP = { country = SOV value = 20 } }
			}

			AND =  {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 }

			}
			AND =  {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 }
				nuked = { country = USA  data = -2 }

			}

				NOT = { nuked = 1 }

				lost_national = { country = USA value = 15 }
			}
			AND =  {
				NOT = { nuked = 1 }
				nuked = { country = USA  data = -5 }
				lost_national = { country = USA value = 5 }
			}
		}
	}
   
	date = { day = 0 month = january year = 1936 }

	}

event = { 
	id = 2049130
	random = no
	country = USA
	
	trigger = {
		ai = no
        policy = { type = professional_army value = 2 } 
	}
       
	name = "Continental Army"
	desc = "The United States mobilized a massive force by the end of world war 2 - numbering over 6 million men in the Army ground forces, with another 2 million in the Army Air Force and 3.4 million in the Navy. In sheer size, the US armed forces rivaled or exceeded the Red Army. However, over half of that force never left the U.S. homeland. The first and primary reason was the unusually high 'tooth-to-tail ratio' of the US army during this time. Partially necessitated by the long logistical chains spread across two oceans, the US Army had an atypically large number of support staff per each individual soldier engaged in actual combat. But there were other reasons as well. One of the chief conerns prior to the war was that the American people are simply not used to prolonged hardship away from home and may not understand the relevance of European or Asian conflicts to their own lives. To rememdy this problem, it was decided to mobilize an extra home army such that soldiers can be drawn from it to serve abroad on a 'rotational basis' should the war fail to end quickly, keeping the US field forces fresh and motivated at all times. Then there were domestic concerns. The long length of the US shorelines required a significant force - not necessarily to defend from an invasion, which was ruled unlikely by 1943, but to intercept raids - which were considered still very much a possibility even very late in the war. The Mexican border was also tightly watched in order to prevent the repeat of the embarassing struggles of the last war. Finally, there was internal security. Much of the US army in fact consisted of the federalized National Guard, which was still badly needed at home as the US struggled with its simmering racial and ethnic tensions. Famously, 6000 Army troops had to be rapidly deployed to Detroit to reinforce the local police department in order to help quell the race riots engulfing the US industrial heartland. To ensure that we don't suffer negative penalties, we must strive to keep at least 150 divisions at home at all times, thoroughly covering the shoreline, the Mexican border, and potential domestic hotspots. Failure to do so may lead to decreased morale, deteriorating supply efficiency, enemy raids, and domestic unrest."
